# Simple Icons (`si`) — 3442 icons

- **Prefix:** `Si` · **Version:** 16.23.0 · **License:** CC0-1.0 · **Style:** fill · **viewBox:** `0 0 24 24`
- **Source:** [simple-icons/simple-icons](https://github.com/simple-icons/simple-icons)
- SVG brand/logo icons for popular companies and tools (e.g. `SiGithub`, `SiReact`, `SiDocker`). Single-color 24×24 fill; theme via `color`.

Import default-only per icon: `import SiExample from "@mal-icons/react/si/SiExample"`, or named from the set barrel: `import { SiExample } from "@mal-icons/react/si"`.
